13:["$","p",null,{"children":"The second run reuses the noise model learned by the first one. A learned noise model belongs to the physical qubits it was learned on, so both runs must place the circuit on the same qubits. TEM transpiles a circuit that is not in the instruction set architecture (ISA) of the device itself, and two transpilations can choose different qubits. Therefore, transpile the circuit one time and pass the same ISA circuit and observable to every run. TEM detects an ISA circuit and does not transpile it again.","className":"group paragraph-el text-text-primary mx-0"}]
